| 35 | `run_command` | Check exact KiCad 10 symbol names | `python3 -c ' import re, pathlib sym_dir = pathlib.Path("/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/symbols") checks = [     ("Amplifier_Operational.kicad_sym", r"TL074"),     ("Device.kicad_sym", r"^(R\|C\|C_Polarized\|C_Polarized_Small\|R_Potentiometer\|LED_Dual_2Pin\|LED_ALT\|LED\|D_Schottky\|D)$"),     ("Diode.kicad_sym", r"BAT54S\|1N4148\|B5819\|1N5819"),     ("Connector_Audio.kicad_sym", r"AudioJack2_Switch"),     ("Connector_Generic.kicad_sym", r"Conn_02x05_Odd_Even"),     ("Connector.kicad_sym", r"^TestPoint$"),     ("Switch.kicad_sym", r"SW_SPST\|SW_SPDT"), ] for fname, pat in checks:     text = (sym_dir / fname).read_text(encoding="utf-8")     names = re.findall(r"^\t\(symbol \"([^\"]+)\"", text, re.M)     matches = [n for n in names if re.search(pat, n)]     print(f"{fname}: {matches}") '` |
| 37 | `run_command` | Inspect exact pin definitions of KiCad symbols | `python3 -c ' import re, pathlib sym_dir = pathlib.Path("/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/symbols")  def dump_sym(lib, sym_name):     text = (sym_dir / f"{lib}.kicad_sym").read_text(encoding="utf-8")     m = re.search(rf"^\t\(symbol \"{re.escape(sym_name)}\".*?(?=^\t\(symbol \|\Z)", text, re.M \| re.S)     if not m:         print(f"MISSING {lib}:{sym_name}")         return     block = m.group(0)     extends = re.search(r"\(extends \"([^\"]+)\"\)", block)     if extends:         print(f"{lib}:{sym_name} extends {extends.group(1)}")         dump_sym(lib, extends.group(1))         return     pins = re.findall(r"\(pin (\w+) \w+.*?\(name \"([^\"]*)\".*?\(number \"([^\"]*)\"", block, re.S)     print(f"{lib}:{sym_name} pins: {pins}")  # Also check all LED symbols in Device dev_text = (sym_dir / "Device.kicad_sym").read_text(encoding="utf-8") print("LED symbols in Device:", [n for n in re.findall(r"^\t\(symbol \"([^\"]+)\"", dev_text, re.M) if "LED" in n])  for lib, sym in [     ("Amplifier_Operational", "TL074"),     ("Diode", "BAT54S"),     ("Diode", "1N4148W"),     ("Device", "D_Schottky"),     ("Device", "C_Polarized"),     ("Device", "R_Potentiometer"),     ("Device", "LED"),     ("Connector_Audio", "AudioJack2_Switch"),     ("Connector_Audio", "AudioJack2_SwitchT"),     ("Connector_Generic", "Conn_02x05_Odd_Even"),     ("Connector", "TestPoint"),     ("Switch", "SW_SPST"),     ("Switch", "SW_SPDT"), ]:     dump_sym(lib, sym) '` |

sys sys.path.insert(0, ".agents/skills/kicad-pcbnew-layout/scripts") import pcbnew from pcbnew_layout_template import parse_netlist  net_path = pathlib.Path("envelope_follower.net") net_text = net_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") assert "NO_LIB" not in net_text, "Found NO_LIB in netlist!"  components, nets = parse_netlist(net_path, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) print(f"Components: {len(components)}, Nets: {len(nets)}") assert len(components) == 80, f"Expected 80 components, got {len(components)}" assert len(nets) == 48, f"Expected 48 nets, got {len(nets)}"  expected_refs = (     {"U1", "U2", "LED1", "J_PWR", "SW1", "SW2"}     \| {f"D{i}" for i in range(1, 6)}     \| {f"R{i}" for i in range(1, 34)}     \| {f"C{i}" for i in range(1, 17)}     \| {f"RV{i}" for i in range(1, 5)}     \| {f"J{i}" for i in range(1, 5)}     \| {f"TP{i}" for i in range(1, 13)} ) assert set(components) == expected_refs, f"Ref mismatch: {set(components) ^ expected_refs}"  for i in range(1, 13):     assert components[f"TP{i}"]["dnp"], f"TP{i} not marked DNP!"  kicad_fp_dir = pathlib.Path("/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/footprints") local_fp_dir = pathlib.Path("footprints")  loaded = {} for ref, comp in components.items():     lib, fp_name = comp["footprint"].split(":", 1)     if lib == "Eurorack_Custom":         lib_path = str(local_fp_dir / f"{lib}.pretty")     else:         lib_path = str(kicad_fp_dir / f"{lib}.pretty")     fp = pcbnew.FootprintLoad(lib_path, fp_name)     assert fp is not None, f"Failed to load {comp[\"footprint\"]} for {ref} from {lib_path}"     loaded[ref] = {pad.GetNumber() for pad in fp.Pads()}  for net in nets:     for ref, pin in net["nodes"]:         assert pin in loaded[ref], f"Missing pad ({ref}, {pin}) in {components[ref][\"footprint\"]} (pads: {sorted(loaded[ref])})"  print("ALL 80 footprints and ALL", sum(len(n["nodes"]) for n in nets), "pin-to-pad connections verified against KiCad 10 + Eurorack_Custom!") '` |

vs {expected_nodes}"  # Check JLCPCB BOM CSV with open("jlcpcb_bom.csv", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:     rows = list(csv.DictReader(f)) assert len(rows) == 17, f"Expected 17 rows in jlcpcb_bom.csv, got {len(rows)}" total_smd = 0 for row in rows:     assert row["Comment"] and row["Designator"] and row["Footprint"] and row["LCSC Part #"]     refs = [r.strip() for r in row["Designator"].split(",")]     total_smd += len(refs) assert total_smd == 50, f"Expected 50 SMD designators in jlcpcb_bom.csv, got {total_smd}"  # Check XML BOM parses and contains custom fields tree = ET.parse("envelope_follower_bom.xml") xml_comps = tree.findall("./components/comp") assert len(xml_comps) == 80  print("ALL CHECKS PASSED: 80 components, 48 exact pin-for-pin nets, 50 SMD in 17 JLCPCB rows, 0 missing pads!") '` |

csv, pathlib, sys, xml.etree.ElementTree as ET sys.path.insert(0, ".agents/skills/kicad-pcbnew-layout/scripts") import pcbnew from pcbnew_layout_template import parse_netlist  erc_text = pathlib.Path("envelope_follower.erc").read_text(encoding="utf-8") assert "No errors or warnings found while running ERC." in erc_text, f"Unexpected ERC: {erc_text}"  net_path = pathlib.Path("envelope_follower.net") net_text = net_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") assert "NO_LIB" not in net_text, "Found NO_LIB in netlist!"  components, nets = parse_netlist(net_path) print("Components:", len(components), "Nets:", len(nets)) assert len(components) == 80 assert len(nets) == 48  expected_refs = (     {"U1", "U2", "LED1", "J_PWR", "SW1", "SW2"}     \| {f"D{i}" for i in range(1, 6)}     \| {f"R{i}" for i in range(1, 34)}     \| {f"C{i}" for i in range(1, 17)}     \| {f"RV{i}" for i in range(1, 5)}     \| {f"J{i}" for i in range(1, 5)}     \| {f"TP{i}" for i in range(1, 13)} ) assert set(components) == expected_refs  for i in range(1, 13):     assert components[f"TP{i}"]["dnp"], f"TP{i} missing dnp property in netlist!" for ref, comp in components.items():     if not ref.startswith("TP"):         assert not comp["dnp"], f"{ref} unexpectedly marked DNP!"  kicad_fp_dir = pathlib.Path("/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/footprints") local_fp_dir = pathlib.Path("footprints")  loaded = {} for ref, comp in components.items():     lib, fp_name = comp["footprint"].split(":", 1)     lib_path = str((local_fp_dir if lib == "Eurorack_Custom" else kicad_fp_dir) / f"{lib}.pretty")     fp = pcbnew.FootprintLoad(lib_path, fp_name)     assert fp is not None, f"Failed to load {lib}:{fp_name} for {ref}"     loaded[ref] = {pad.GetNumber() for pad in fp.Pads()}  for net in nets:     for ref, pin in net["nodes"]:         fp_id = components[ref]["footprint"]         assert pin in loaded[ref], f"Missing pad ({ref}, {pin}) in {fp_id} (pads: {sorted(loaded[ref])})"  spec_nets = {     "NET_PWR_POS_RAW": {("J_PWR", "9"), ("J_PWR", "10"), ("D1", "2")},     "NET_PWR_NEG_RAW": {("J_PWR", "1"), ("J_PWR", "2"), ("D2", "1")},     "NET_POS_PROT": {("D1", "1"), ("R1", "1")},     "NET_NEG_PROT": {("D2", "2"), ("R2", "1")},     "+12V": {("R1", "2"), ("C1", "1"), ("C3", "1"), ("C5", "1"), ("U1", "4"), ("U2", "4"), ("R26", "1"), ("TP2", "1")},     "-12V": {("R2", "2"), ("C2", "2"), ("C4", "1"), ("C6", "1"), ("U1", "11"), ("U2", "11"), ("R28", "1"), ("TP3", "1")},     "GND": {         ("J_PWR", "3"), ("J_PWR", "4"), ("J_PWR", "5"), ("J_PWR", "6"), ("J_PWR", "7"), ("J_PWR", "8"),         ("C1", "2"), ("C2", "1"), ("C3", "2"), ("C4", "2"), ("C5", "2"), ("C6", "2"),         ("J1", "S"), ("J1", "TN"), ("R3", "2"), ("RV1", "1"), ("R5", "2"), ("C9", "2"),         ("U1", "5"), ("U1", "10"), ("J2", "S"), ("R16", "2"), ("C11", "2"), ("C12", "2"),         ("C13", "2"), ("C14", "2"), ("U2", "5"), ("C15", "2"), ("C16", "2"), ("J4", "S"),         ("U2", "10"), ("J3", "S"), ("R33", "2"), ("TP1", "1"), ("TP12", "1"),     },     "NET_IN_JACK": {("J1", "T"), ("R3", "1"), ("C7", "1")},     "NET_IN_AC": {("C7", "2"), ("RV1", "3")},     "NET_GAIN_WIPER": {("RV1", "2"), ("R4", "1")},     "NET_U1A_POS": {("R4", "2"), ("R5", "1"), ("U1", "3")},     "NET_U1A_INV": {("U1", "2"), ("R6", "1"), ("C8", "1"), ("R7", "1")},     "NET_U1A_CG": {("R7", "2"), ("C9", "1")},     "NET_PREAMP_OUT": {("U1", "1"), ("R6", "2"), ("C8", "2"), ("R8", "1"), ("R11", "1"), ("TP4", "1")},     "NET_FF_MID": {("R11", "2"), ("R12", "1")},     "NET_U1B_INV": {("R8", "2"), ("U1", "6"), ("R9", "1"), ("D3", "2")},     "NET_U1B_OUT": {("U1", "7"), ("D3", "3")},     "NET_RECT_HALF": {("D3", "1"), ("R9", "2"), ("R10", "1"), ("TP9", "1")},     "NET_RECT_SUM": {("R10", "2"), ("R12", "2"), ("U1", "9"), ("R13", "1")},     "NET_FWR_FB_MID": {("R13", "2"), ("R14", "1")},     "NET_RECT_OUT": {("U1", "8"), ("R14", "2"), ("R15", "1"), ("TP5", "2"), ("C10", "1"), ("U1", "13")},     "NET_U1D_OUT": {("U1", "14"), ("C10", "2"), ("D4", "2"), ("D5", "1"), ("TP10", "1")},     "NET_ATK_DIODE": {("D4", "1"), ("R19", "1")},     "NET_ATK_MIN": {("R19", "2"), ("RV2", "1")},     "NET_REL_DIODE": {("D5", "2"), ("R20", "1")},     "NET_REL_MIN": {("R20", "2"), ("RV3", "1")},     "NET_ENV_CAP": {("RV2", "2"), ("RV2", "3"), ("RV3", "2"), ("RV3", "3"), ("C11", "1"), ("SW2", "2"), ("U2", "3")},     "NET_SLOW_CAPS": {("SW2", "3"), ("C12", "1"), ("C13", "1")},     "NET_ENV_BUF": {("U2", "1"), ("U2", "2"), ("R18", "1"), ("R21", "1"), ("TP6", "1")},     "NET_SMOOTH_MID": {("R21", "2"), ("R22", "1"), ("SW1", "2")},     "NET_SMOOTH_SW": {("SW1", "3"), ("C14", "1")},     "NET_OFF_POS_FILT": {("R26", "2"), ("C15", "1"), ("R27", "1")},     "NET_OFF_TOP": {("R27", "2"), ("RV4", "3")},     "NET_OFF_NEG_FILT": {("R28", "2"), ("C16", "1"), ("R29", "1")},     "NET_OFF_BOT": {("R29", "2"), ("RV4", "1")},     "NET_OFF_WIPER": {("RV4", "2"), ("R23", "1"), ("TP11", "1")},     "NET_U2B_INV": {("R22", "2"), ("R23", "2"), ("R24", "1"), ("U2", "6")},     "NET_INV_AMP": {("U2", "7"), ("R24", "2"), ("R25", "1"), ("R30", "1"), ("TP7", "1")},     "NET_INV_OUT_JACK": {("R25", "2"), ("J4", "T")},     "NET_U2C_INV": {("R30", "2"), ("R31", "1"), ("U2", "9")},     "NET_OUT_AMP": {("U2", "8"), ("R31", "2"), ("R32", "1"), ("U2", "12"), ("TP8", "1")},     "NET_OUT_JACK": {("R32", "2"), ("J3", "T")},     "NET_LED_DRV": {("U2", "14"), ("LED1", "1")},     "NET_LED_SENSE": {("U2", "13"), ("LED1", "2"), ("R33", "1")}, } net_map = {n["name"]: set(n["nodes"]) for n in nets} assert set(net_map) == set(spec_nets) for name, expected_nodes in spec_nets.items():     assert net_map[name] == expected_nodes, f"Net {name} mismatch"  with open("jlcpcb_bom.csv", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:     rows = list(csv.DictReader(f)) assert len(rows) == 17, f"Expected 17 rows, got {len(rows)}" total_smd = 0 for row in rows:     assert row["Comment"] and row["Designator"] and row["Footprint"] and row["LCSC Part #"]     total_smd += len([r.strip() for r in row["Designator"].split(",")]) assert total_smd == 50, f"Expected 50 SMD parts, got {total_smd}"  ext_lcsc = {     components[r]["fields"]["LCSC"]     for r, c in components.items()     if c["fields"].get("JLCPCB_Class") == "Extended" } assert ext_lcsc == {"C2878713", "C83935", "C95204"}, f"Unexpected Extended SKUs: {ext_lcsc}"  tree = ET.parse("envelope_follower_bom.xml") xml_comps = tree.findall("./components/comp") xml_nets = tree.findall("./nets/net") assert len(xml_comps) == 80, f"Expected 80 XML comps, got {len(xml_comps)}" assert len(xml_nets) == 48, f"Expected 48 XML nets, got {len(xml_nets)}" lcsc_comps = [c for c in xml_comps if c.find("./fields/field[@name=\"LCSC\"]") is not None] assert len(lcsc_comps) == 62, f"Expected 62 comps with LCSC (50 SMD + 12 TP), got {len(lcsc_comps)}" dnp_comps = {c.attrib["ref"] for c in xml_comps if c.find("./property[@name=\"dnp\"]") is not None} assert dnp_comps == {f"TP{i}" for i in range(1, 13)}, f"Unexpected DNP XML comps: {dnp_comps}"  print("ALL CHECKS PASSED: ERC clean, 80 comps, 48 exact pin-for-pin nets, 80 footprints/pads, 17 CSV rows (50 SMD, 3 Extended SKUs), valid XML BOM!") | 232 | `run_command` | Extract exact pin geometry for all schematic symbols | `python3 -c ' import pathlib, re sym_dir = pathlib.Path("/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/symbols")  def extract_sym(lib, name):     text = (sym_dir / f"{lib}.kicad_sym").read_text(encoding="utf-8")     m = re.search(rf"^\t\(symbol \"{re.escape(name)}\".*?(?=^\t\(symbol \|\Z)", text, re.M \| re.S)     assert m, f"Missing {lib}:{name}"     block = m.group(0)     ext = re.search(r"\(extends \"([^\"]+)\"\)", block)     if ext:         return extract_sym(lib, ext.group(1))     return block  syms = [     ("Amplifier_Operational", "TL074"),     ("Device", "D_Schottky"),     ("Diode", "BAT54S"),     ("Diode", "1N4148W"),     ("Device", "R"),     ("Device", "C"),     ("Device", "C_Polarized"),     ("Device", "R_Potentiometer"),     ("Switch", "SW_SPDT"),     ("Device", "LED_Dual_Bidirectional"),     ("Connector_Audio", "AudioJack2_SwitchT"),     ("Connector_Generic", "Conn_02x05_Odd_Even"),     ("Connector", "TestPoint"),     ("power", "+12V"),     ("power", "-12V"),     ("power", "GND"),     ("power", "PWR_FLAG"), ] for lib, name in syms:     blk = extract_sym(lib, name)     # Find each subsymbol unit and its pins     units = re.findall(r"\(symbol \"[^\"]+_(\d+)_(\d+)\"(.*?)(?=\(symbol \"\|\(embedded_fonts\|\Z)", blk, re.S)     print(f"=== {lib}:{name} ===")     for u_num, style, u_blk in units:         pins = re.findall(r"\(pin (\w+) \w+\s+\(at ([\d.-]+) ([\d.-]+) ([\d.-]+)\)\s+\(length ([\d.-]+)\).*?\(name \"([^\"]*)\".*?\(number \"([^\"]*)\"", u_blk, re.S)         if pins:             print(f"  unit {u_num} (style {style}):")             for ptype, x, y, ang, ln, pname, pnum in pins:                 print(f"    pin {pnum:>2} ({pname:>4}, {ptype:>8}): at=({x}, {y}) ang={ang} len={ln}") '` |

| 242 | `run_command` | Measure symbol body extents for collision-free schematic layout | `python3 -c ' import pathlib, re sym_dir = pathlib.Path("/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/symbols")  def extract_sym(lib, name):     text = (sym_dir / f"{lib}.kicad_sym").read_text(encoding="utf-8")     m = re.search(rf"^\t\(symbol \"{re.escape(name)}\".*?(?=^\t\(symbol \|\Z)", text, re.M \| re.S)     block = m.group(0)     ext = re.search(r"\(extends \"([^\"]+)\"\)", block)     if ext:         return extract_sym(lib, ext.group(1))     return block  for lib, name in [     ("Amplifier_Operational", "TL074"),     ("Device", "R"),     ("Device", "C"),     ("Device", "C_Polarized"),     ("Device", "D_Schottky"),     ("Diode", "BAT54S"),     ("Device", "R_Potentiometer"),     ("Switch", "SW_SPDT"),     ("Device", "LED_Dual_Bidirectional"),     ("Connector_Audio", "AudioJack2_SwitchT"),     ("Connector_Generic", "Conn_02x05_Odd_Even"),     ("Connector", "TestPoint"), ]:     blk = extract_sym(lib, name)     coords = re.findall(r"\((?:xy\|start\|end\|center)\s+([\d.-]+)\s+([\d.-]+)\)", blk)     xs = [float(x) for x, _ in coords]     ys = [float(y) for _, y in coords]     print(f"{lib}:{name:24s} body_x=[{min(xs):6.2f}, {max(xs):6.2f}] body_y=[{min(ys):6.2f}, {max(ys):6.2f}]") '` |

| 257 | `run_command` | Test KiCad 10 symbol flattening with simp_sexp | `.venv/bin/python -c ' import math, pathlib, re, subprocess from simp_sexp import Sexp  sym_dir = pathlib.Path("/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/symbols")  def load_sym(lib, name):     text = (sym_dir / f"{lib}.kicad_sym").read_text(encoding="utf-8")     tree = Sexp(text)     syms = {s[1]: s for s in tree if isinstance(s, Sexp) and len(s) > 1 and s[0] == "symbol"}     def flatten(sym_name):         sym = syms[sym_name]         ext = None         for item in sym[2:]:             if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] == "extends":                 ext = str(item[1])                 break         if not ext:             return sym         parent = flatten(ext)         child_props = {item[1]: item for item in sym[2:] if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] == "property"}         merged = ["symbol", sym_name]         # header flags from child (fallback parent)         child_flags = [item for item in sym[2:] if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] not in ("extends", "property", "symbol")]         parent_flags = [item for item in parent[2:] if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] not in ("extends", "property", "symbol")]         merged.extend(child_flags if child_flags else parent_flags)         seen_props = set()         for item in parent[2:]:             if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] == "property":                 pname = item[1]                 seen_props.add(pname)                 merged.append(child_props.get(pname, item))         for item in sym[2:]:             if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] == "property" and item[1] not in seen_props:                 merged.append(item)         # sub-symbols from parent (renamed) + any from child         for item in parent[2:]:             if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] == "symbol":                 sub = Sexp(str(item))                 sub[1] = sub[1].replace(f"{ext}_", f"{sym_name}_", 1)                 merged.append(sub)         return Sexp(merged)     res = flatten(name)     res[1] = f"{lib}:{name}"     return res  for l, n in [("Amplifier_Operational", "TL074"), ("Diode", "1N4148W"), ("power", "+12V")]:     s = load_sym(l, n)     print(l, n, "subsyms:", [x[1] for x in s[2:] if isinstance(x, Sexp) and x[0] == "symbol"]) '` |

pathlib.Path("/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/symbols")  def load_sym(lib, name):     tree = Sexp((sym_dir / f"{lib}.kicad_sym").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))     syms = {s[1]: s for s in tree if isinstance(s, Sexp) and len(s) > 1 and s[0] == "symbol"}     def flatten(sym_name):         sym = syms[sym_name]         ext = next((str(i[1]) for i in sym[2:] if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] == "extends"), None)         if not ext:             return sym         parent = flatten(ext)         child_props = {i[1]: i for i in sym[2:] if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] == "property"}         merged = ["symbol", sym_name]         c_flags = [i for i in sym[2:] if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] not in ("extends", "property", "symbol")]         p_flags = [i for i in parent[2:] if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] not in ("extends", "property", "symbol")]         merged.extend(c_flags if c_flags else p_flags)         seen = set()         for i in parent[2:]:             if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] == "property":                 seen.add(i[1])                 merged.append(child_props.get(i[1], i))         for i in sym[2:]:             if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] == "property" and i[1] not in seen:                 merged.append(i)         for i in parent[2:]:             if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] == "symbol":                 sub = Sexp(str(i))                 sub[1] = sub[1].replace(f"{ext}_", f"{sym_name}_", 1)                 merged.append(sub)         return Sexp(merged)     res = flatten(name)     res[1] = f"{lib}:{name}"     return res  def get_pins(sym):     pins = {}     for sub in sym[2:]:         if isinstance(sub, Sexp) and sub[0] == "symbol":             for item in sub[2:]:                 if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] == "pin":                     at = next(x for x in item if isinstance(x, Sexp) and x[0] == "at")                     num = next(x for x in item if isinstance(x, Sexp) and x[0] == "number")                     pins[str(num[1])] = (float(at[1]), float(at[2]))     return pins  def pin_pos(pins, num, sx, sy, rot=0, mirror=None):     px, py = pins[str(num)]     if mirror == "x":         py = -py     elif mirror == "y":         px = -px     rad = math.radians(rot)     rx = px * math.cos(rad) - py * math.sin(rad)     ry = px * math.sin(rad) + py * math.cos(rad)     return round(sx + rx, 4), round(sy - ry, 4)  j_sym = load_sym("Connector_Audio", "AudioJack2_SwitchT") d_sym = load_sym("Diode", "BAT54S") r_sym = load_sym("Device", "R") jp = get_pins(j_sym) dp = get_pins(d_sym) rp = get_pins(r_sym)  # Test J1 mirror x at (50, 50), J2 rot 180 at (100, 50), D3 mirror y at (150, 50), R1 rot 90 at (50, 100) lines = [     "(kicad_sch (version 20250114) (generator \"test\") (generator_version \"1.0\") (uuid 11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111) (paper \"A4\")",     "(lib_symbols",     j_sym.to_str(),     d_sym.to_str(),     r_sym.to_str(),     ")", ] def place(lib_id, ref, x, y, rot=0, mirror=None, pnums=()):     mir = f"(mirror {mirror})" if mirror else ""     pstr = " ".join(f"(pin \"{n}\" (uuid 22222222-2222-4222-8222-{ref.encode().hex():0<12}))" for i, n in enumerate(pnums))     return f"""(symbol (lib_id "{lib_id}") (at {x} {y} {rot}) {mir} (unit 1) (exclude_from_sim no) (in_bom yes) (on_board yes) (dnp no) (uuid 33333333-3333-4333-8333-{ref.encode().hex():0<12})       (property "Reference" "{ref}" (at {x} {y} 0) (effects (font (size 1.27 1.27))))       (property "Value" "{ref}" (at {x} {y} 0) (effects (font (size 1.27 1.27))))       (property "Footprint" "" (at {x} {y} 0) (effects (font (size 1.27 1.27)) (hide yes)))       (property "Datasheet" "" (at {x} {y} 0) (effects (font (size 1.27 1.27)) (hide yes)))       (instances (project "test" (path "/11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111" (reference "{ref}") (unit 1))))     )""" lines.append(place("Connector_Audio:AudioJack2_SwitchT", "J1", 50.8, 50.8, 0, "x", ["S","T","TN"])) lines.append(place("Connector_Audio:AudioJack2_SwitchT", "J2", 101.6, 50.8, 180, None, ["S","T","TN"])) lines.append(place("Diode:BAT54S", "D3", 152.4, 50.8, 0, "y", ["1","2","3"])) lines.append(place("Device:R", "R1", 50.8, 101.6, 90, None, ["1","2"]))  # Label each pin at its computed pin_pos! for name, pts in [     ("J1_T", pin_pos(jp, "T", 50.8, 50.8, 0, "x")),     ("J1_S", pin_pos(jp, "S", 50.8, 50.8, 0, "x")),     ("J2_T", pin_pos(jp, "T", 101.6, 50.8, 180, None)),     ("J2_S", pin_pos(jp, "S", 101.6, 50.8, 180, None)),     ("D3_1", pin_pos(dp, "1", 152.4, 50.8, 0, "y")),     ("D3_2", pin_pos(dp, "2", 152.4, 50.8, 0, "y")),     ("D3_3", pin_pos(dp, "3", 152.4, 50.8, 0, "y")),     ("R1_1", pin_pos(rp, "1", 50.8, 101.6, 90, None)),     ("R1_2", pin_pos(rp, "2", 50.8, 101.6, 90, None)), ]:     px, py = pts     lines.append(f"(wire (pts (xy {px} {py}) (xy {px+2.54} {py})) (stroke (width 0) (type default)))")     lines.append(f"(label \"{name}\" (at {px+2.54} {py} 0) (effects (font (size 1.27 1.27))))")  lines.append("(sheet_instances (path \"/\" (page \"1\")))") lines.append(")") pathlib.Path("/tmp/sch_xform_test.kicad_sch").write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8") subprocess.run(["/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/MacOS/kicad-cli", "sch", "export", "netlist", "--output", "/tmp/sch_xform_test.net", "/tmp/sch_xform_test.kicad_sch"], check=True) print(pathlib.Path("/tmp/sch_xform_test.net").read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("(nets")[1]) '` |

 if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] == "property":                 seen.add(i[1])                 merged.append(child_props.get(i[1], i))         for i in sym[2:]:             if isinstance(i, Sexp) and      pins[str(num[1])] = (float(at[1]), float(at[2]))     return pins  def pin_pos(pins, num, sx, sy, rot=0, mirror=None):     px, py = pins[str(num)]     if mirror == "x":         py = -py     elif []     i, n = 0, len(text)     while i < n:         ch = text[i]         if ch.isspace():             i += 1         elif ch in ("(", ")"):             tokens.append(ch)             i += 1         elif ch == "\"":             i += 1             buf = []             while i < n:                 c = text[i]                 if c == "\\":                     i += 1                     if i < n:                         esc = text[i]                         buf.append({"n": "\n", "r": "\r", "t": "\t"}.get(esc, esc))                         i += 1                 elif c == "\"":                     i += 1                     break                 else:                     buf.append(c)                     i += 1             tokens.append(Quoted("".join(buf)))         else:             j = i             while j < n and not text[j].isspace() and text[j] not in ("(", ")"):                 j += 1             tokens.append(text[i:j])             i = j     stack = [[]]     for tok in tokens:         if tok == "(":             lst = []             stack[-1].append(lst)             stack.append(lst)         elif tok == ")":             stack.pop()         else:             stack[-1].append(tok)     return stack[0][0]  def clone_node(node):     if isinstance(node, list):         return [clone_node(x) for x in node]     return node  def dump_sexpr(node, indent=0):     if isinstance(node, Quoted):         escaped = str(node).replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("\"", "\\\"").replace("\n", "\\n")         return f"\"{escaped}\""     if not isinstance(node, list):         return str(node)     if not node:         return "()"     if all(not isinstance(x, list) for x in node):         return "(" + " ".join(dump_sexpr(x) for x in node) + ")"     pad = "  " * indent     head = [dump_sexpr(node[0])]     rest = []     for item in node[1:]:         if isinstance(item, list):             rest.append("\n" + "  " * (indent + 1) + dump_sexpr(item, indent + 1))         else:             head.append(dump_sexpr(item))     return "(" + " ".join(head) + "".join(rest) + ")"  sym_dir = pathlib.Path("/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/symbols")  def load_sym(lib, name):         sym = syms[sym_name]         ext = next((str(i[1]) for i in sym[2:] if isinstance(i, list) and i[0] == "extends"), None)         if not ext:             return clone_node(sym)         parent = flatten(ext)         child_props = {i[1]: clone_node(i) for i in sym[2:] if isinstance(i, list) and i[0] == "property"}         merged = ["symbol", Quoted(sym_name)]         c_flags = [clone_node(i) for i in sym[2:] if isinstance(i, list) and i[0] not in ("extends", "property", "symbol")]         p_flags = [clone_node(i) for i in parent[2:] if isinstance(i, list) and i[0] not in ("extends", "property", "symbol")]         merged.extend(c_flags if c_flags else p_flags)         seen = set()         for i in parent[2:]:             if isinstance(i, list) and i[0] == "property":                 seen.add(i[1])                 merged.append(child_props.get(i[1], clone_node(i)))         for i in sym[2:]:             if isinstance(i, list) and i[0] == "property" and i[1] not in seen:                 merged.append(clone_node(i))         for i in parent[2:]:             if isinstance(i, list) and i[0] == "symbol":                 sub = clone_node(i)                 sub[1] = Quoted(str(sub[1]).replace(f"{ext}_", f"{sym_name}_", 1))                 merged.append(sub)         return merged     res = flatten(name)     res[1] = Quoted(f"{lib}:{name}")     # Strip KiCad 10 .kicad_sym-only tokens if incompatible with schematic parser? if isinstance(item, list) and item[0] == "pin":                     at = next(x for x in item if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "at")                     num = next(x for x in item if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "number")                     pins[str(num[1])] = (float(at[1]), float(at[2]))     return pins  def pin_pos(pins, num, sx, sy, rot=0, mirror=None):     px, py = pins[str(num)]     if mirror == "x":         py = -py     elif mirror == "y":         px = -px     rad = math.radians(rot)     rx = px * math.cos(rad) - py * math.sin(rad)     ry = px * math.sin(rad) + py * math.cos(rad)     return round(sx + rx, 4), round(sy - ry, 4)  j_sym = load_sym("Connector_Audio", "AudioJack2_SwitchT") d_sym = load_sym("Diode", "BAT54S") r_sym = load_sym("Device", "R") u_sym = | 297 | `run_command` | Compare schematic exported netlist against SKiDL netlist | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".agents/skills/kicad-pcbnew-layout/scripts") from pcbnew_layout_template import parse_netlist  ref_comps, ref_nets = parse_netlist(pathlib.Path("envelope_follower.net")) sch_comps, sch_nets = parse_netlist(pathlib.Path("/tmp/sch_exported.net"))  # Filter out #PWR* and #FLG* sch_real = {r: c for r, c in sch_comps.items() if not r.startswith("#")} print("Ref comps:", len(ref_comps), "Sch real comps:", len(sch_real)) if set(ref_comps) != set(sch_real):     print("COMP DIFF:", set(ref_comps) ^ set(sch_real)) for r in sorted(set(ref_comps) & set(sch_real)):     if ref_comps[r]["value"] != sch_real[r]["value"] or ref_comps[r]["footprint"] != sch_real[r]["footprint"] or ref_comps[r]["dnp"] != sch_real[r]["dnp"]:         print(f"ATTR DIFF {r}: ref=({ref_comps[r][\"value\"]},{ref_comps[r][\"footprint\"]},{ref_comps[r][\"dnp\"]}) vs sch=({sch_real[r][\"value\"]},{sch_real[r][\"footprint\"]},{sch_real[r][\"dnp\"]})")  ref_map = {n["name"]: set(n["nodes"]) for n in ref_nets} sch_map = {n["name"].lstrip("/"): {(r, p) for r, p in n["nodes"] if not r.startswith("#")} for n in sch_nets} sch_map = {k: v for k, v in sch_map.items() if v}  print("Ref nets:", len(ref_map), "Sch nets:", len(sch_map)) if set(ref_map) != set(sch_map):     print("ONLY IN REF:", sorted(set(ref_map) - set(sch_map)))     print("ONLY IN SCH:", sorted(set(sch_map) - set(ref_map))) for name in sorted(set(ref_map) & set(sch_map)):     if ref_map[name] != sch_map[name]:         print(f"NET {name} DIFF: missing={ref_map[name]-sch_map[name]} extra={sch_map[name]-ref_map[name]}") '` |
| 300 | `run_command` | Check netlist parity details between SKiDL and schematic | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".agents/skills/kicad-pcbnew-layout/scripts") from pcbnew_layout_template import parse_netlist  ref_comps, ref_nets = parse_netlist(pathlib.Path("envelope_follower.net")) sch_comps, sch_nets = parse_netlist(pathlib.Path("/tmp/sch_exported.net"))  sch_real = {r: c for r, c in sch_comps.items() if not r.startswith("#")} print("Ref comps:", len(ref_comps), "Sch real comps:", len(sch_real)) if set(ref_comps) != set(sch_real):     print("COMP DIFF:", set(ref_comps) ^ set(sch_real)) for r in sorted(set(ref_comps) & set(sch_real)):     rc, sc = ref_comps[r], sch_real[r]     if rc["value"] != sc["value"] or rc["footprint"] != sc["footprint"] or rc["dnp"] != sc["dnp"]:         print("ATTR DIFF", r, (rc["value"], rc["footprint"], rc["dnp"]), "vs", (sc["value"], sc["footprint"], sc["dnp"]))  ref_map = {n["name"]: set(n["nodes"]) for n in ref_nets} sch_map = {n["name"].lstrip("/"): {(r, p) for r, p in n["nodes"] if not r.startswith("#")} for n in sch_nets} sch_map = {k: v for k, v in sch_map.items() if v}  print("Ref nets:", len(ref_map), "Sch nets:", len(sch_map)) if set(ref_map) != set(sch_map):     print("ONLY IN REF:", sorted(set(ref_map) - set(sch_map)))     print("ONLY IN SCH:", sorted(set(sch_map) - set(ref_map))) for name in sorted(set(ref_map) & set(sch_map)):     if ref_map[name] != sch_map[name]:         print("NET", name, "DIFF: missing=", ref_map[name]-sch_map[name], "extra=", sch_map[name]-ref_map[name]) '` |

| 315 | `run_command` | Verify 100% netlist parity against envelope_follower.net | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".agents/skills/kicad-pcbnew-layout/scripts") from pcbnew_layout_template import parse_netlist  ref_comps, ref_nets = parse_netlist(pathlib.Path("envelope_follower.net")) sch_comps, sch_nets = parse_netlist(pathlib.Path("/tmp/sch_exported.net"))  sch_real = {r: c for r, c in sch_comps.items() if not r.startswith("#")} print("Ref comps:", len(ref_comps), "Sch real comps:", len(sch_real)) assert set(ref_comps) == set(sch_real), f"COMP DIFF: {set(ref_comps) ^ set(sch_real)}" for r in sorted(ref_comps):     rc, sc = ref_comps[r], sch_real[r]     assert rc["value"] == sc["value"] and rc["footprint"] == sc["footprint"] and rc["dnp"] == sc["dnp"], (         f"ATTR DIFF {r}: {(rc[\"value\"], rc[\"footprint\"], rc[\"dnp\"])} vs {(sc[\"value\"], sc[\"footprint\"], sc[\"dnp\"])}"     )  ref_map = {n["name"]: set(n["nodes"]) for n in ref_nets} sch_map = {n["name"].lstrip("/"): {(r, p) for r, p in n["nodes"] if not r.startswith("#")} for n in sch_nets} sch_map = {k: v for k, v in sch_map.items() if v}  print("Ref nets:", len(ref_map), "Sch nets:", len(sch_map)) if set(ref_map) != set(sch_map):     print("ONLY IN REF:", sorted(set(ref_map) - set(sch_map)))     print("ONLY IN SCH:", sorted(set(sch_map) - set(ref_map))) for name in sorted(set(ref_map) & set(sch_map)):     if ref_map[name] != sch_map[name]:         print("NET", name, "DIFF: missing=", ref_map[name]-sch_map[name], "extra=", sch_map[name]-ref_map[name]) if set(ref_map) == set(sch_map) and all(ref_map[k] == sch_map[k] for k in ref_map):     print("100% NETLIST PARITY ACHIEVED: 80 components and 48 nets match pin-for-pin!") '` |
| 318 | `run_command` | Verify 100% netlist parity and check ERC warning types | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".agents/skills/kicad-pcbnew-layout/scripts") from pcbnew_layout_template import parse_netlist  ref_comps, ref_nets = parse_netlist(pathlib.Path("envelope_follower.net")) sch_comps, sch_nets = parse_netlist(pathlib.Path("/tmp/sch_exported.net"))  sch_real = {r: c for r, c in sch_comps.items() if not r.startswith("#")} print("Ref comps:", len(ref_comps), "Sch real comps:", len(sch_real)) assert set(ref_comps) == set(sch_real), f"COMP DIFF: {set(ref_comps) ^ set(sch_real)}" for r in sorted(ref_comps):     rc, sc = ref_comps[r], sch_real[r]     rv, sv = rc["value"], sc["value"]     rf, sf = rc["footprint"], sc["footprint"]     rd, sd = rc["dnp"], sc["dnp"]     assert (rv, rf, rd) == (sv, sf, sd), f"ATTR DIFF {r}: {(rv, rf, rd)} vs {(sv, sf, sd)}"  ref_map = {n["name"]: set(n["nodes"]) for n in ref_nets} sch_map = {n["name"].lstrip("/"): {(r, p) for r, p in n["nodes"] if not r.startswith("#")} for n in sch_nets} sch_map = {k: v for k, v in sch_map.items() if v}  print("Ref nets:", len(ref_map), "Sch nets:", len(sch_map)) if set(ref_map) != set(sch_map):     print("ONLY IN REF:", sorted(set(ref_map) - set(sch_map)))     print("ONLY IN SCH:", sorted(set(sch_map) - set(ref_map))) for name in sorted(set(ref_map) & set(sch_map)):     if ref_map[name] != sch_map[name]:         print("NET", name, "DIFF: missing=", ref_map[name]-sch_map[name], "extra=", sch_map[name]-ref_map[name]) assert set(ref_map) == set(sch_map) and all(ref_map[k] == sch_map[k] for k in ref_map) print("100% NETLIST PARITY ACHIEVED: 80 components and 48 nets match pin-for-pin!") ' echo "=== Check ERC non-lib warning types ===" grep "^\[" /tmp/sch_erc.rpt \| sort \| uniq -c` |

| 374 | `run_command` | Verify schematic netlist parity and ERC summary | `/Users/brett/Documents/GitHub/EnvelopeFollowerWithSkewLimiter/.venv/bin/python - <<'EOF' import pathlib, collections, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_schematic as gs  # 1. Summarize ERC report erc = pathlib.Path("/tmp/sch_erc.rpt").read_text() print("=== ERC HEADER & TYPES ===") for line in erc.splitlines():     if line.startswith(("ERC report", "**", "*****", "[")) or "Errors:" in line:         print(line)  # 2. Parse both netlists via gs.parse_sexpr def parse_net(path):     tree = gs.parse_sexpr(pathlib.Path(path).read_text())     comps = {}     nets = {}     for item in tree:         if isinstance(item, list) and item[0] == "components":             for c in item[1:]:                 if isinstance(c, list) and c[0] == "comp":                     ref = str(next(x[1] for x in c[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "ref"))                     val = str(next(x[1] for x in c[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "value"))                     fp = str(next((x[1] for x in c[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "footprint"), ""))                     dnp = False                     props = [x for x in c[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "property"]                     for p in props:                         if str(p[1]) == "dnp":                             dnp = True                     comps[ref] = {"value": val, "footprint": fp, "dnp": dnp}         elif isinstance(item, list) and item[0] == "nets":             for n in item[1:]:                 if isinstance(n, list) and n[0] == "net":                     name = str(next(x[1] for x in n[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "name"))                     nodes = set()                     for x in n[1:]:                         if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "node":                             nref = str(next(y[1] for y in x[1:] if isinstance(y, list) and y[0] == "ref"))                             npin = str(next(y[1] for y in x[1:] if isinstance(y, list) and y[0] == "pin"))                             nodes.add((nref, npin))                     nets[name] = nodes     return comps, nets  c_sk, n_sk = parse_net("envelope_follower.net") c_sch, n_sch = parse_net("/tmp/sch_exported.net")  real_sch = {k: v for k, v in c_sch.items() if not k.startswith("#")} print(f"\nComponents: skidl={len(c_sk)}, schematic_real={len(real_sch)}") comp_mismatches = [] for ref in sorted(set(c_sk) \| set(real_sch)):     if c_sk.get(ref) != real_sch.get(ref):         comp_mismatches.append((ref, c_sk.get(ref), real_sch.get(ref))) print(f"Component mismatches: {len(comp_mismatches)}") for m in comp_mismatches:     print(" ", m)  # Filter #PWR/#FLG pins from schematic nets, and skip unconnected- NC nets def filter_nodes(nodes):     return {(r, p) for r, p in nodes if not r.startswith("#")}  print(f"\nNets: skidl={len(n_sk)}, schematic={len(n_sch)}") unconn = {k: sorted(v) for k, v in n_sch.items() if k.startswith("unconnected-")} print("Unconnected NC nets:", unconn)  # Compare net sets (allowing unnamed or / prefixed net names if any) sk_map = {k: filter_nodes(v) for k, v in n_sk.items() if filter_nodes(v)} sch_map = {k.lstrip("/"): filter_nodes(v) for k, v in n_sch.items() if not k.startswith("unconnected-") and filter_nodes(v)} net_mismatches = [] for name in sorted(set(sk_map) \| set(sch_map)):     if sk_map.get(name) != sch_map.get(name):         net_mismatches.append((name, sorted(sk_map.get(name, set()) - sch_map.get(name, set())), sorted(sch_map.get(name, set()) - sk_map.get(name, set())))) print(f"Net mismatches: {len(net_mismatches)}") for m in net_mismatches[:20]:     print(" ", m) EOF` |

   if isinstance(c, list) and c[0] == "comp":                     ref = str(next(x[1] for x in c[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "ref"))                     val = str(next(x[1] for x in c[1:]                 nodes = {(str(next(y[1] for y in x[1:] if isinstance(y, list) and y[0] == "ref")),                               str(next(y[1] for y in x[1:] if isinstance(y, list) and y[0] == sch_map = {k.lstrip("/"): fn(v) for k, v in n_sch.items() if not k.startswith("unconnected-") and fn(v)} net_diff = [n for n in sorted(set(sk_map) \| set(sch_map)) if sk_map.get(n) != sch_map.get(n)] | 37 | Inspect exact pin definitions of KiCad symbols | `python3 -c ' import re, pathlib sym_dir = pathlib.Path("/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/symbols")  def dump_sym(lib, sym_name):     text = (sym_dir / f"{lib}.kicad_sym").read_text(encoding="utf-8")     m = re.search(rf"^\t\(symbol \"{re.escape(sym_name)}\".*?(?=^\t\(symbol \|\Z)", text, re.M \| re.S)     if not m:         print(f"MISSING {lib}:{sym_name}")         return     block = m.group(0)     extends = re.search(r"\(extends \"([^\"]+)\"\)", block)     if extends:         print(f"{lib}:{sym_name} extends {extends.group(1)}")         dump_sym(lib, extends.group(1))         return     pins = re.findall(r"\(pin (\w+) \w+.*?\(name \"([^\"]*)\".*?\(number \"([^\"]*)\"", block, re.S)     print(f"{lib}:{sym_name} pins: {pins}")  # Also check all LED symbols in Device dev_text = (sym_dir / "Device.kicad_sym").read_text(encoding="utf-8") print("LED symbols in Device:", [n for n in re.findall(r"^\t\(symbol \"([^\"]+)\"", dev_text, re.M) if "LED" in n])  for lib, sym in [     ("Amplifier_Operational", "TL074"),     ("Diode", "BAT54S"),     ("Diode", "1N4148W"),     ("Device", "D_Schottky"),     ("Device", "C_Polarized"),     ("Device", "R_Potentiometer"),     ("Device", "LED"),     ("Connector_Audio", "AudioJack2_Switch"),     ("Connector_Audio", "AudioJack2_SwitchT"),     ("Connector_Generic", "Conn_02x05_Odd_Even"),     ("Connector", "TestPoint"),     ("Switch", "SW_SPST"),     ("Switch", "SW_SPDT"), ]:     dump_sym(lib, sym) '` |

| 107 | Test SKiDL part ref naming and pin slice behavior | `.venv/bin/python -c ' import os, builtins os.environ["XDG_DATA_HOME"] = "/tmp/xdg_data" os.environ["MPLCONFIGDIR"] = "/tmp/mpl_config" os.environ["KICAD10_SYMBOL_DIR"] = "/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/symbols" os.environ["KICAD_SYMBOL_DIR"] = "/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/symbols" from skidl import Part, Net, TEMPLATE led = Part("Device", "LED_Dual_Bidirectional", ref="LED1", value="A-1076", footprint="LED_THT:LED_D3.0mm", tag="led1") jpwr = Part("Connector_Generic", "Conn_02x05_Odd_Even", ref="J_PWR", value="EURORACK_PWR", footprint="Connector_IDC:IDC-Header_2x05_P2.54mm_Vertical", tag="j_pwr") rv1 = Part("Device", "R_Potentiometer", ref="RV1", value="A100K", footprint="Potentiometer_THT:Potentiometer_Alpha_RD901F-40-00D_Single_Vertical", tag="rv1") sw1 = Part("Switch", "SW_SPDT", ref="SW1", value="SMOOTH", footprint="Eurorack_Custom:SW_Toggle_SPDT_Dailywell_2MS1", tag="sw1") tp1 = Part("Connector", "TestPoint", ref="TP1", value="GND", footprint="TestPoint:TestPoint_Keystone_5000-5004_Miniature", tag="tp1") print("refs:", led.ref, jpwr.ref, rv1.ref, sw1.ref, tp1.ref) print("jpwr pins 3:8:", [p.num for p in jpwr[3:8]], "vs list:", [p.num for p in jpwr[3,4,5,6,7,8]]) '` |

sys.path.insert(0, ".agents/skills/kicad-pcbnew-layout/scripts") import pcbnew from pcbnew_layout_template import parse_netlist  net_path = pathlib.Path("envelope_follower.net") net_text = net_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") assert "NO_LIB" not in net_text, "Found NO_LIB in netlist!"  components, nets = parse_netlist(net_path, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) print(f"Components: {len(components)}, Nets: {len(nets)}") assert len(components) == 80, f"Expected 80 components, got {len(components)}" assert len(nets) == 48, f"Expected 48 nets, got {len(nets)}"  expected_refs = (     {"U1", "U2", "LED1", "J_PWR", "SW1", "SW2"}     \| {f"D{i}" for i in range(1, 6)}     \| {f"R{i}" for i in range(1, 34)}     \| {f"C{i}" for i in range(1, 17)}     \| {f"RV{i}" for i in range(1, 5)}     \| {f"J{i}" for i in range(1, 5)}     \| {f"TP{i}" for i in range(1, 13)} ) assert set(components) == expected_refs, f"Ref mismatch: {set(components) ^ expected_refs}"  for i in range(1, 13):     assert components[f"TP{i}"]["dnp"], f"TP{i} not marked DNP!"  kicad_fp_dir = pathlib.Path("/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/footprints") local_fp_dir = pathlib.Path("footprints")  loaded = {} for ref, comp in components.items():     lib, fp_name = comp["footprint"].split(":", 1)     if lib == "Eurorack_Custom":         lib_path = str(local_fp_dir / f"{lib}.pretty")     else:         lib_path = str(kicad_fp_dir / f"{lib}.pretty")     fp = pcbnew.FootprintLoad(lib_path, fp_name)     assert fp is not None, f"Failed to load {comp[\"footprint\"]} for {ref} from {lib_path}"     loaded[ref] = {pad.GetNumber() for pad in fp.Pads()}  for net in nets:     for ref, pin in net["nodes"]:         assert pin in loaded[ref], f"Missing pad ({ref}, {pin}) in {components[ref][\"footprint\"]} (pads: {sorted(loaded[ref])})"  print("ALL 80 footprints and ALL", sum(len(n["nodes"]) for n in nets), "pin-to-pad connections verified against KiCad 10 + Eurorack_Custom!") '` |

| 232 | Extract exact pin geometry for all schematic symbols | `python3 -c ' import pathlib, re sym_dir = pathlib.Path("/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/symbols")  def extract_sym(lib, name):     text = (sym_dir / f"{lib}.kicad_sym").read_text(encoding="utf-8")     m = re.search(rf"^\t\(symbol \"{re.escape(name)}\".*?(?=^\t\(symbol \|\Z)", text, re.M \| re.S)     assert m, f"Missing {lib}:{name}"     block = m.group(0)     ext = re.search(r"\(extends \"([^\"]+)\"\)", block)     if ext:         return extract_sym(lib, ext.group(1))     return block  syms = [     ("Amplifier_Operational", "TL074"),     ("Device", "D_Schottky"),     ("Diode", "BAT54S"),     ("Diode", "1N4148W"),     ("Device", "R"),     ("Device", "C"),     ("Device", "C_Polarized"),     ("Device", "R_Potentiometer"),     ("Switch", "SW_SPDT"),     ("Device", "LED_Dual_Bidirectional"),     ("Connector_Audio", "AudioJack2_SwitchT"),     ("Connector_Generic", "Conn_02x05_Odd_Even"),     ("Connector", "TestPoint"),     ("power", "+12V"),     ("power", "-12V"),     ("power", "GND"),     ("power", "PWR_FLAG"), ] for lib, name in syms:     blk = extract_sym(lib, name)     # Find each subsymbol unit and its pins     units = re.findall(r"\(symbol \"[^\"]+_(\d+)_(\d+)\"(.*?)(?=\(symbol \"\|\(embedded_fonts\|\Z)", blk, re.S)     print(f"=== {lib}:{name} ===")     for u_num, style, u_blk in units:         pins = re.findall(r"\(pin (\w+) \w+\s+\(at ([\d.-]+) ([\d.-]+) ([\d.-]+)\)\s+\(length ([\d.-]+)\).*?\(name \"([^\"]*)\".*?\(number \"([^\"]*)\"", u_blk, re.S)         if pins:             print(f"  unit {u_num} (style {style}):")             for ptype, x, y, ang, ln, pname, pnum in pins:                 print(f"    pin {pnum:>2} ({pname:>4}, {ptype:>8}): at=({x}, {y}) ang={ang} len={ln}") '` |
| 235 | Inspect KiCad 10 .kicad_sch structure from schematizer output | `python3 -c ' import pathlib, re text = pathlib.Path("/tmp/sch_probe_flat/envelope_follower.kicad_sch").read_text(encoding="utf-8") print("=== Header (lines 1-25) ===") print("\n".join(text.splitlines()[:25])) print("\n=== Embedded lib_symbol names ===") lib_start = text.index("(lib_symbols") inst_start = text.index("\n\t(symbol\n") if "\n\t(symbol\n" in text else text.index("\n  (symbol\n") lib_sec = text[lib_start:inst_start] for m in re.finditer(r"\(symbol \"([^\"]+:[^\"]+)\"", lib_sec):     print(" ", m.group(1)) print("\n=== Sample placed symbol (U1 and TP1 and R1) ===") for ref in ["U1", "TP1", "R1", "SW1", "J3"]:     m = re.search(rf"\(symbol\s+\(lib_id[^\n]+\n(?:.*?\n){{1,35}}?\s*\(property \"Reference\" \"{ref}\".*?(?=\n\t\(symbol\|\n  \(symbol\|\n\t\(wire\|\n  \(wire\|\Z)", text[inst_start:], re.S)     if m:         print(f"--- {ref} ---")         print(m.group(0)[:700]) '` |

| 242 | Measure symbol body extents for collision-free schematic layout | `python3 -c ' import pathlib, re sym_dir = pathlib.Path("/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/symbols")  def extract_sym(lib, name):     text = (sym_dir / f"{lib}.kicad_sym").read_text(encoding="utf-8")     m = re.search(rf"^\t\(symbol \"{re.escape(name)}\".*?(?=^\t\(symbol \|\Z)", text, re.M \| re.S)     block = m.group(0)     ext = re.search(r"\(extends \"([^\"]+)\"\)", block)     if ext:         return extract_sym(lib, ext.group(1))     return block  for lib, name in [     ("Amplifier_Operational", "TL074"),     ("Device", "R"),     ("Device", "C"),     ("Device", "C_Polarized"),     ("Device", "D_Schottky"),     ("Diode", "BAT54S"),     ("Device", "R_Potentiometer"),     ("Switch", "SW_SPDT"),     ("Device", "LED_Dual_Bidirectional"),     ("Connector_Audio", "AudioJack2_SwitchT"),     ("Connector_Generic", "Conn_02x05_Odd_Even"),     ("Connector", "TestPoint"), ]:     blk = extract_sym(lib, name)     coords = re.findall(r"\((?:xy\|start\|end\|center)\s+([\d.-]+)\s+([\d.-]+)\)", blk)     xs = [float(x) for x, _ in coords]     ys = [float(y) for _, y in coords]     print(f"{lib}:{name:24s} body_x=[{min(xs):6.2f}, {max(xs):6.2f}] body_y=[{min(ys):6.2f}, {max(ys):6.2f}]") '` |

| 257 | Test KiCad 10 symbol flattening with simp_sexp | `.venv/bin/python -c ' import math, pathlib, re, subprocess from simp_sexp import Sexp  sym_dir = pathlib.Path("/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/SharedSupport/symbols")  def load_sym(lib, name):     text = (sym_dir / f"{lib}.kicad_sym").read_text(encoding="utf-8")     tree = Sexp(text)     syms = {s[1]: s for s in tree if isinstance(s, Sexp) and len(s) > 1 and s[0] == "symbol"}     def flatten(sym_name):         sym = syms[sym_name]         ext = None         for item in sym[2:]:             if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] == "extends":                 ext = str(item[1])                 break         if not ext:             return sym         parent = flatten(ext)         child_props = {item[1]: item for item in sym[2:] if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] == "property"}         merged = ["symbol", sym_name]         # header flags from child (fallback parent)         child_flags = [item for item in sym[2:] if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] not in ("extends", "property", "symbol")]         parent_flags = [item for item in parent[2:] if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] not in ("extends", "property", "symbol")]         merged.extend(child_flags if child_flags else parent_flags)         seen_props = set()         for item in parent[2:]:             if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] == "property":                 pname = item[1]                 seen_props.add(pname)                 merged.append(child_props.get(pname, item))         for item in sym[2:]:             if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] == "property" and item[1] not in seen_props:                 merged.append(item)         # sub-symbols from parent (renamed) + any from child         for item in parent[2:]:             if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] == "symbol":                 sub = Sexp(str(item))                 sub[1] = sub[1].replace(f"{ext}_", f"{sym_name}_", 1)                 merged.append(sub)         return Sexp(merged)     res = flatten(name)     res[1] = f"{lib}:{name}"     return res  for l, n in [("Amplifier_Operational", "TL074"), ("Diode", "1N4148W"), ("power", "+12V")]:     s = load_sym(l, n)     print(l, n, "subsyms:", [x[1] for x in s[2:] if isinstance(x, Sexp) and x[0] == "symbol"]) '` |

for s in tree if isinstance(s, Sexp) and len(s) > 1 and s[0] == "symbol"}     def flatten(sym_name):         sym = syms[sym_name]         ext = next((str(i[1]) for i in sym[2:] if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] == "extends"), None)         if not ext:             return sym         parent = flatten(ext)         child_props = {i[1]: i for i in sym[2:] if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] == "property"}         merged = ["symbol", sym_name]         c_flags = [i for i in sym[2:] if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] not in ("extends", "property", "symbol")]         p_flags = [i for i in parent[2:] if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] not in ("extends", "property", "symbol")]         merged.extend(c_flags if c_flags else p_flags)         seen = set()         for i in parent[2:]:             if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] == "property":                 seen.add(i[1])                 merged.append(child_props.get(i[1], i))         for i in sym[2:]:             if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] == "property" and i[1] not in seen:                 merged.append(i)         for i in parent[2:]:             if isinstance(i, Sexp) and i[0] == "symbol":                 sub = Sexp(str(i))                 sub[1] = sub[1].replace(f"{ext}_", f"{sym_name}_", 1)                 merged.append(sub)         return Sexp(merged)     res = flatten(name)     res[1] = f"{lib}:{name}"     return res  def get_pins(sym):     pins = {}     for sub in sym[2:]:         if isinstance(sub, Sexp) and sub[0] == "symbol":             for item in sub[2:]:                 if isinstance(item, Sexp) and item[0] == "pin":                     at = next(x for x in item if isinstance(x, Sexp) and x[0] == "at")                     num = next(x for x in item if isinstance(x, Sexp) and x[0] == "number")                     pins[str(num[1])] = (float(at[1]), float(at[2]))     return pins  def pin_pos(pins, num, sx, sy, rot=0, mirror=None):     px, py = pins[str(num)]     if mirror == "x":         py = -py     elif mirror == "y":         px = -px     rad = math.radians(rot)     rx = px * math.cos(rad) - py * math.sin(rad)     ry = px * math.sin(rad) + py * math.cos(rad)     return round(sx + rx, 4), round(sy - ry, 4)  j_sym = load_sym("Connector_Audio", "AudioJack2_SwitchT") d_sym = load_sym("Diode", "BAT54S") r_sym = load_sym("Device", "R") jp = get_pins(j_sym) dp = get_pins(d_sym) rp = get_pins(r_sym)  # Test J1 mirror x at (50, 50), J2 rot 180 at (100, 50), D3 mirror y at (150, 50), R1 rot 90 at (50, 100) lines = [     "(kicad_sch (version 20250114) (generator \"test\") (generator_version \"1.0\") (uuid 11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111) (paper \"A4\")",     "(lib_symbols",     j_sym.to_str(),     d_sym.to_str(),     r_sym.to_str(),     ")", ] def place(lib_id, ref, x, y, rot=0, mirror=None, pnums=()):     mir = f"(mirror {mirror})" if mirror else ""     pstr = " ".join(f"(pin \"{n}\" (uuid 22222222-2222-4222-8222-{ref.encode().hex():0<12}))" for i, n in enumerate(pnums))     return f"""(symbol (lib_id "{lib_id}") (at {x} {y} {rot}) {mir} (unit 1) (exclude_from_sim no) (in_bom yes) (on_board yes) (dnp no) (uuid 33333333-3333-4333-8333-{ref.encode().hex():0<12})       (property "Reference" "{ref}" (at {x} {y} 0) (effects (font (size 1.27 1.27))))       (property "Value" "{ref}" (at {x} {y} 0) (effects (font (size 1.27 1.27))))       (property "Footprint" "" (at {x} {y} 0) (effects (font (size 1.27 1.27)) (hide yes)))       (property "Datasheet" "" (at {x} {y} 0) (effects (font (size 1.27 1.27)) (hide yes)))       (instances (project "test" (path "/11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111" (reference "{ref}") (unit 1))))     )""" lines.append(place("Connector_Audio:AudioJack2_SwitchT", "J1", 50.8, 50.8, 0, "x", ["S","T","TN"])) lines.append(place("Connector_Audio:AudioJack2_SwitchT", "J2", 101.6, 50.8, 180, None, ["S","T","TN"])) lines.append(place("Diode:BAT54S", "D3", 152.4, 50.8, 0, "y", ["1","2","3"])) lines.append(place("Device:R", "R1", 50.8, 101.6, 90, None, ["1","2"]))  # Label each pin at its computed pin_pos! for name, pts in [     ("J1_T", pin_pos(jp, "T", 50.8, 50.8, 0, "x")),     ("J1_S", pin_pos(jp, "S", 50.8, 50.8, 0, "x")),     ("J2_T", pin_pos(jp, "T", 101.6, 50.8, 180, None)),     ("J2_S", pin_pos(jp, "S", 101.6, 50.8, 180, None)),     ("D3_1", pin_pos(dp, "1", 152.4, 50.8, 0, "y")),     ("D3_2", pin_pos(dp, "2", 152.4, 50.8, 0, "y")),     ("D3_3", pin_pos(dp, "3", 152.4, 50.8, 0, "y")),     ("R1_1", pin_pos(rp, "1", 50.8, 101.6, 90, None)),     ("R1_2", pin_pos(rp, "2", | 297 | Compare schematic exported netlist against SKiDL netlist | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".agents/skills/kicad-pcbnew-layout/scripts") from pcbnew_layout_template import parse_netlist  ref_comps, ref_nets = parse_netlist(pathlib.Path("envelope_follower.net")) sch_comps, sch_nets = parse_netlist(pathlib.Path("/tmp/sch_exported.net"))  # Filter out #PWR* and #FLG* sch_real = {r: c for r, c in sch_comps.items() if not r.startswith("#")} print("Ref comps:", len(ref_comps), "Sch real comps:", len(sch_real)) if set(ref_comps) != set(sch_real):     print("COMP DIFF:", set(ref_comps) ^ set(sch_real)) for r in sorted(set(ref_comps) & set(sch_real)):     if ref_comps[r]["value"] != sch_real[r]["value"] or ref_comps[r]["footprint"] != sch_real[r]["footprint"] or ref_comps[r]["dnp"] != sch_real[r]["dnp"]:         print(f"ATTR DIFF {r}: ref=({ref_comps[r][\"value\"]},{ref_comps[r][\"footprint\"]},{ref_comps[r][\"dnp\"]}) vs sch=({sch_real[r][\"value\"]},{sch_real[r][\"footprint\"]},{sch_real[r][\"dnp\"]})")  ref_map = {n["name"]: set(n["nodes"]) for n in ref_nets} sch_map = {n["name"].lstrip("/"): {(r, p) for r, p in n["nodes"] if not r.startswith("#")} for n in sch_nets} sch_map = {k: v for k, v in sch_map.items() if v}  print("Ref nets:", len(ref_map), "Sch nets:", len(sch_map)) if set(ref_map) != set(sch_map):     print("ONLY IN REF:", sorted(set(ref_map) - set(sch_map)))     print("ONLY IN SCH:", sorted(set(sch_map) - set(ref_map))) for name in sorted(set(ref_map) & set(sch_map)):     if ref_map[name] != sch_map[name]:         print(f"NET {name} DIFF: missing={ref_map[name]-sch_map[name]} extra={sch_map[name]-ref_map[name]}") '` |
| 300 | Check netlist parity details between SKiDL and schematic | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".agents/skills/kicad-pcbnew-layout/scripts") from pcbnew_layout_template import parse_netlist  ref_comps, ref_nets = parse_netlist(pathlib.Path("envelope_follower.net")) sch_comps, sch_nets = parse_netlist(pathlib.Path("/tmp/sch_exported.net"))  sch_real = {r: c for r, c in sch_comps.items() if not r.startswith("#")} print("Ref comps:", len(ref_comps), "Sch real comps:", len(sch_real)) if set(ref_comps) != set(sch_real):     print("COMP DIFF:", set(ref_comps) ^ set(sch_real)) for r in sorted(set(ref_comps) & set(sch_real)):     rc, sc = ref_comps[r], sch_real[r]     if rc["value"] != sc["value"] or rc["footprint"] != sc["footprint"] or rc["dnp"] != sc["dnp"]:         print("ATTR DIFF", r, (rc["value"], rc["footprint"], rc["dnp"]), "vs", (sc["value"], sc["footprint"], sc["dnp"]))  ref_map = {n["name"]: set(n["nodes"]) for n in ref_nets} sch_map = {n["name"].lstrip("/"): {(r, p) for r, p in n["nodes"] if not r.startswith("#")} for n in sch_nets} sch_map = {k: v for k, v in sch_map.items() if v}  print("Ref nets:", len(ref_map), "Sch nets:", len(sch_map)) if set(ref_map) != set(sch_map):     print("ONLY IN REF:", sorted(set(ref_map) - set(sch_map)))     print("ONLY IN SCH:", sorted(set(sch_map) - set(ref_map))) for name in sorted(set(ref_map) & set(sch_map)):     if ref_map[name] != sch_map[name]:         print("NET", name, "DIFF: missing=", ref_map[name]-sch_map[name], "extra=", sch_map[name]-ref_map[name]) '` |

| 318 | Verify 100% netlist parity and check ERC warning types | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".agents/skills/kicad-pcbnew-layout/scripts") from pcbnew_layout_template import parse_netlist  ref_comps, ref_nets = parse_netlist(pathlib.Path("envelope_follower.net")) sch_comps, sch_nets = parse_netlist(pathlib.Path("/tmp/sch_exported.net"))  sch_real = {r: c for r, c in sch_comps.items() if not r.startswith("#")} print("Ref comps:", len(ref_comps), "Sch real comps:", len(sch_real)) assert set(ref_comps) == set(sch_real), f"COMP DIFF: {set(ref_comps) ^ set(sch_real)}" for r in sorted(ref_comps):     rc, sc = ref_comps[r], sch_real[r]     rv, sv = rc["value"], sc["value"]     rf, sf = rc["footprint"], sc["footprint"]     rd, sd = rc["dnp"], sc["dnp"]     assert (rv, rf, rd) == (sv, sf, sd), f"ATTR DIFF {r}: {(rv, rf, rd)} vs {(sv, sf, sd)}"  ref_map = {n["name"]: set(n["nodes"]) for n in ref_nets} sch_map = {n["name"].lstrip("/"): {(r, p) for r, p in n["nodes"] if not r.startswith("#")} for n in sch_nets} sch_map = {k: v for k, v in sch_map.items() if v}  print("Ref nets:", len(ref_map), "Sch nets:", len(sch_map)) if set(ref_map) != set(sch_map):     print("ONLY IN REF:", sorted(set(ref_map) - set(sch_map)))     print("ONLY IN SCH:", sorted(set(sch_map) - set(ref_map))) for name in sorted(set(ref_map) & set(sch_map)):     if ref_map[name] != sch_map[name]:         print("NET", name, "DIFF: missing=", ref_map[name]-sch_map[name], "extra=", sch_map[name]-ref_map[name]) assert set(ref_map) == set(sch_map) and all(ref_map[k] == sch_map[k] for k in ref_map) print("100% NETLIST PARITY ACHIEVED: 80 components and 48 nets match pin-for-pin!") ' echo "=== Check ERC non-lib warning types ===" grep "^\[" /tmp/sch_erc.rpt \| sort \| uniq -c` |

| 374 | Verify schematic netlist parity and ERC summary | `/Users/brett/Documents/GitHub/EnvelopeFollowerWithSkewLimiter/.venv/bin/python - <<'EOF' import pathlib, collections, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_schematic as gs  # 1. Summarize ERC report erc = pathlib.Path("/tmp/sch_erc.rpt").read_text() print("=== ERC HEADER & TYPES ===") for line in erc.splitlines():     if line.startswith(("ERC report", "**", "*****", "[")) or "Errors:" in line:         print(line)  # 2. Parse both netlists via gs.parse_sexpr def parse_net(path):     tree = gs.parse_sexpr(pathlib.Path(path).read_text())     comps = {}     nets = {}     for item in tree:         if isinstance(item, list) and item[0] == "components":             for c in item[1:]:                 if isinstance(c, list) and c[0] == "comp":                     ref = str(next(x[1] for x in c[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "ref"))                     val = str(next(x[1] for x in c[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "value"))                     fp = str(next((x[1] for x in c[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "footprint"), ""))                     dnp = False                     props = [x for x in c[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "property"]                     for p in props:                         if str(p[1]) == "dnp":                             dnp = True                     comps[ref] = {"value": val, "footprint": fp, "dnp": dnp}         elif isinstance(item, list) and item[0] == "nets":             for n in item[1:]:                 if isinstance(n, list) and n[0] == "net":                     name = str(next(x[1] for x in n[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "name"))                     nodes = set()                     for x in n[1:]:                         if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "node":                             nref = str(next(y[1] for y in x[1:] if isinstance(y, list) and y[0] == "ref"))                             npin = str(next(y[1] for y in x[1:] if isinstance(y, list) and y[0] == "pin"))                             nodes.add((nref, npin))                     nets[name] = nodes     return comps, nets  c_sk, n_sk = parse_net("envelope_follower.net") c_sch, n_sch = parse_net("/tmp/sch_exported.net")  real_sch = {k: v for k, v in c_sch.items() if not k.startswith("#")} print(f"\nComponents: skidl={len(c_sk)}, schematic_real={len(real_sch)}") comp_mismatches = [] for ref in sorted(set(c_sk) \| set(real_sch)):     if c_sk.get(ref) != real_sch.get(ref):         comp_mismatches.append((ref, c_sk.get(ref), real_sch.get(ref))) print(f"Component mismatches: {len(comp_mismatches)}") for m in comp_mismatches:     print(" ", m)  # Filter #PWR/#FLG pins from schematic nets, and skip unconnected- NC nets def filter_nodes(nodes):     return {(r, p) for r, p in nodes if not r.startswith("#")}  print(f"\nNets: skidl={len(n_sk)}, schematic={len(n_sch)}") unconn = {k: sorted(v) for k, v in n_sch.items() if k.startswith("unconnected-")} print("Unconnected NC nets:", unconn)  # Compare net sets (allowing unnamed or / prefixed net names if any) sk_map = {k: filter_nodes(v) for k, v in n_sk.items() if filter_nodes(v)} sch_map = {k.lstrip("/"): filter_nodes(v) for k, v in n_sch.items() if not k.startswith("unconnected-") and filter_nodes(v)} net_mismatches = [] for name in sorted(set(sk_map) \| set(sch_map)):     if sk_map.get(name) != sch_map.get(name):         net_mismatches.append((name, sorted(sk_map.get(name, set()) - sch_map.get(name, set())), sorted(sch_map.get(name, set()) - sk_map.get(name, set())))) print(f"Net mismatches: {len(net_mismatches)}") for m in net_mismatches[:20]:     print(" ", m) EOF` |

| 382 | Inspect analyzer output and render PNG crops | `python3 - <<'EOF' import json, pathlib, subprocess  data = json.loads(pathlib.Path("/tmp/sch_analysis.json").read_text()) print("Analyzer keys:", list(data.keys())) print("Findings summary:") for k, v in data.items():     if k == "findings":         print(f"  findings ({len(v)}):")         for f in v:             print("   ", f.get("id"), f.get("severity"), f.get("title") or f.get("message"))     elif k in ("trust_summary", "summary", "statistics", "erc_warnings", "validation_errors"):         print(f"  {k}:", v)  subprocess.run(["sips", "-s", "format", "png", "-Z", "2800", "/tmp/sch_view/envelope_follower.pdf", "--out", "/tmp/sch_view/full.png"], check=True) # Check pixel dimensions out = subprocess.check_output(["sips", "-g", "pixelWidth", "-g", "pixelHeight", "/tmp/sch_view/full.png"], text=True) print(out) pw = int([l.split()[-1] for l in out.splitlines() if "pixelWidth" in l][0]) ph = int([l.split()[-1] for l in out.splitlines() if "pixelHeight" in l][0])  # Create same 5 crops crops = [     ("crop_row1_left.png",  0,          0,          int(ph * 0.40), int(pw * 0.70)),     ("crop_row1_right.png", int(pw*0.65), 0,        int(ph * 0.40), int(pw * 0.35)),     ("crop_row2_left.png",  0,          int(ph*0.36), int(ph*0.36), int(pw * 0.65)),     ("crop_row2_right.png", int(pw*0.46), int(ph*0.36), int(ph*0.36), int(pw * 0.54)),     ("crop_row3.png",       0,          int(ph*0.69), int(ph*0.31), int(pw * 0.78)), ] for name, x, y, h, w in crops:     dst = f"/tmp/sch_view/{name}"     subprocess.run(["sips", "-c", str(h), str(w), "--cropOffset", str(y), str(x), "/tmp/sch_view/full.png", "--out", dst], check=True) EOF` |

".") import generate_schematic as gs  # Render PNG crops using PIL cleanly (exact quadrants!) subprocess.run(["sips", "-s", "format", "png", "-Z", "2800", "/tmp/sch_view/envelope_follower.pdf", "--out", "/tmp/sch_view/full.png"], check=True) from PIL import Image im = Image.open("/tmp/sch_view/full.png") w, h = im.size im.crop((0, 0, int(w * 0.72), int(h * 0.42))).save("/tmp/sch_view/sec1_2.png") im.crop((int(w * 0.65), 0, w, int(h * 0.42))).save("/tmp/sch_view/sec3.png") im.crop((0, int(h * 0.36), int(w * 0.65), int(h * 0.72))).save("/tmp/sch_view/sec4_5.png") im.crop((int(w * 0.46), int(h * 0.36), w, int(h * 0.72))).save("/tmp/sch_view/sec5_6.png") im.crop((0, int(h * 0.69), int(w * 0.78), h)).save("/tmp/sch_view/sec7_8_9.png")  # Check (project ...) in envelope_follower.kicad_sch and run netlist parity check sch_txt = pathlib.Path("envelope_follower.kicad_sch").read_text() print("First instance block:") for ln in sch_txt.splitlines():     if "instances" in ln or "project" in ln:         print(" ", ln)         if "project" in ln:             break  def parse_net(path):     tree = gs.parse_sexpr(pathlib.Path(path).read_text())     comps, nets = {}, {}     for item in tree:         if isinstance(item, list) and item[0] == "components":             for c in item[1:]:                 if isinstance(c, list) and c[0] == "comp":                     ref = str(next(x[1] for x in c[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "ref"))                     val = str(next(x[1] for x in c[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "value"))                     fp = str(next((x[1] for x in c[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "footprint"), ""))                     dnp = any(str(p[1]) == "dnp" for p in c[1:] if isinstance(p, list) and p[0] == "property")                     comps[ref] = {"value": val, "footprint": fp, "dnp": dnp}         elif isinstance(item, list) and item[0] == "nets":             for n in item[1:]:                 if isinstance(n, list) and n[0] == "net":                     name = str(next(x[1] for x in n[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "name"))                     nodes = {(str(next(y[1] for y in x[1:] if isinstance(y, list) and y[0] == "ref")),                               str(next(y[1] for y in x[1:] if isinstance(y, list) and y[0] == "pin")))                              for x in n[1:] if isinstance(x, list) and x[0] == "node"}                     nets[name] = nodes     return comps, nets  c_sk, n_sk = parse_net("envelope_follower.net") c_sch, n_sch = parse_net("/tmp/sch_exported.net") real_sch = {k: v for k, v in c_sch.items() if not k.startswith("#")} comp_diff = [(r, c_sk.get(r), real_sch.get(r)) for r in sorted(set(c_sk) \| set(real_sch)) if c_sk.get(r) != real_sch.get(r)] fn = lambda s: {(r, p) for r, p in s if not r.startswith("#")} sk_map = {k: fn(v) for k, v in n_sk.items() if fn(v)} sch_map = {k.lstrip("/"): fn(v) for k, v in n_sch.items() if not k.startswith("unconnected-") and fn(v)} net_diff = [n for n in sorted(set(sk_map) \| set(sch_map)) if sk_map.get(n) != sch_map.get(n)] print(f"PARITY: comps={len(real_sch)}/{len(c_sk)} mismatches={len(comp_diff)}, nets={len(sch_map)}/{len(sk_map)} mismatches={len(net_diff)}") assert len(comp_diff) == 0 and len(net_diff) == 0 EOF` |
